Results

MOFA-CORE execution flow

This tab follows the execution order in MOFA-CORE.R. The first steps build R objects in memory: metadata tables, cleaned view matrices, annotations, normalised MOFA inputs, and the trained model object. Later steps write files and directories under Results/.

When run_analysis = TRUE, this report sources the function definitions from MOFA-CORE.R and executes this workflow directly. For output-producing steps, skip_existing = TRUE means the step is skipped when the mapped output files or directories already exist. This avoids rerunning plotting and enrichment functions when the result tree is already populated.

Workflow plan

Execution plan derived from the MOFA-CORE.R workflow and expected output locations.
Order Phase Function ExpectedOutput Status Description
1 Environment setup_project_environment object step Set up the R package environment and load required packages.
2 Import import_view_metadata object step Read the workbook view metadata that defines the analysis views.
3 Import import_sample_metadata object step Read and transpose the sample metadata.
4 Import load_and_clean_input_data object step Read, clean, and align all active data sheets.
5 Annotation generate_lipid_annotations Annotations/Lipid.annotations.csv present Generate or load lipid annotations.
6 Annotation generate_kegg_annotation_lists Annotations/kegg_annotation_cache present Generate or load KEGG TERM2GENE and TERM2NAME resources.
7 Annotation annotate_transcriptomic_data_with_kegg Annotations/KEGG_gene_annotations.csv present Map transcriptomic features to KEGG-compatible gene identifiers.
8 Annotation annotate_metabolomic_data_with_kegg Annotations/KEGG_metabolite_annotations.csv present Map metabolomic features to KEGG compound identifiers.
9 Annotation load_or_build_fella_database Annotations/fella_db present Load or build FELLA pathway graph resources.
10 Normalisation normalise_data_mofa_v1_or_v2 object step Normalise each active view into MOFA-ready matrices.
11 Model initialise_and_run_mofa Results/Full_MOFA_model.hdf5 present Create, train, or load the MOFA model.
12 Metadata generate_sample_metadata object step Derive sample categories from sample names.
13 Metadata assign_sample_metadata_to_mofa object step Attach sample metadata to the MOFA model.
14 Metadata define_group_colours object step Define group colour palettes.
15 Metadata define_view_colours object step Define view colour palettes.
16 Post-processing extract_kegg_weights object step Attach KEGG-weight annotations to the MOFA object.
17 Post-processing run_limma_statistics object step Run limma statistics for factor interpretation.
18 Post-processing standardise_feature_names object step Create a plotting-safe MOFA object with standardised feature names.
19 Global outputs generate_mofa_variance_plots Results/Corrilation_and_Variance_plots/Variance_explained.png present Write global variance-explained outputs.
20 Global outputs generate_mofa_factor_matrix_plot Results/PCA/category_Factor_Pair_PCA_Matrix.png present Write PCA/factor projection outputs.
21 Global outputs generate_mofa_factor_comparison_plot Results/Covariate_factor_comparisons/category_Factor_Comparison.png present Write covariate and group comparison outputs.
22 Global outputs generate_mofa_factor_correlation_plot Results/Corrilation_and_Variance_plots/Factor_vs_Covariate_corrilation.png present Write factor correlation and covariate-correlation outputs.
23 Global outputs save_weight_tables Results/Significance_testing/tables/Weights_AllViews_AllFactors__category.csv present Write combined factor weight tables.
24 Global outputs plot_scatter_pval_vs_weight Results/Significance_testing/regression_testing/R2_scatter__category.png present Write significance-versus-weight scatter and regression outputs.
25 Global outputs plot_violin_pval_vs_weight Results/Significance_testing/violin_plots/Violin__category.png present Write significance-related violin plots.
26 Global outputs plot_top_weights_heatmap_faceted_by_view Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/TopWeights_Heatmap_FacetedByView.png present Write faceted top-weight heatmap.
27 Per-factor outputs plot_top_weights_view Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor//Top_Weights.png present Write per-view top-weight plots for each factor.
28 Per-factor outputs plot_top_weights_per_factor Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor*/Top-weights-bar.png present Write factor-level top-weight bar plots.
29 Per-factor outputs plot_scatter_per_view Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor//scatterplots present Write per-view scatter plots for each factor.
30 Per-factor outputs plot_heatmap_per_view Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor//Heatmap.png present Write per-view heatmaps for each factor.
31 Per-factor outputs create_weight_outputs Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor*/All_Weights.csv present Write per-factor weight tables and top-feature tables.
32 Per-factor outputs perform_kegg_enrichment / plot_enrichment_network / generate_simple_pathway_graph / run_fella_enrichment Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor/KEGG_Enrichment_Weighted.csv; Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor/Top_100_edges_KEGG_cnet.png; Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor//KEGG_pathway_plots; Results/Per_Factor_Analysis/Factor//FELLA_Enrichment_*.csv present Write KEGG, pathway, enrichment-network, and FELLA outputs for each factor.

Analysis information

Methods

Analytical framework

This report documents a reusable multi-omics factor analysis workflow. The workbook contains multiple assay views. The workflow aligns those views by sample, normalises the matrices, and fits a MOFA model. MOFA learns latent factors: hidden axes of variation that summarise coordinated structure across the data views.

The interpretation layer then asks three practical questions:

  • Which samples and sample groups are separated by each factor?
  • Which features carry the strongest positive or negative weights on each factor?
  • Which pathways or biological networks are enriched among the strongest factor-associated features?

Input processing

The workbook is controlled by view_metadata and sample_metadata. view_metadata identifies the active views and provides the view-level metadata needed for import and annotation. sample_metadata provides sample-level information used for normalisation and factor comparison.

Each active data sheet is read into R, cleaned, and added to a named list of view matrices. The workflow expects feature identifiers in the first column and sample IDs in the remaining columns. The same sample layout must be maintained across active views.

Annotation resources

The workflow uses cached annotation resources where possible. Lipid names are standardised with Goslin/rgoslin logic. KEGG resources are used to connect genes and metabolites to pathways. FELLA resources provide pathway-network and diffusion-based enrichment support.

The cached Annotations/ tree is important for reproducibility. If it is removed, the workflow may need network access and regenerated pathway resources may differ if external databases have changed.

MOFA modelling

The normalised view matrices are passed to MOFA. The trained model is saved as Results/Full_MOFA_model.hdf5. Global outputs describe variance explained, factor correlations, and covariate associations. Per-factor outputs then turn each latent factor into feature-level and pathway-level summaries.

When run_analysis = TRUE, this report does not call a project-specific runner as a single black-box process. It sources the function definitions from MOFA-CORE.R, builds the in-memory objects in the same execution order, and maps output-generating functions to the files and directories they write.

Output interpretation

High absolute feature weights identify features that contribute strongly to a factor. Positive and negative weights represent opposite directions along the same latent axis. Enrichment outputs should be read as interpretation aids rather than independent validation of the factor. The most robust interpretation comes from reading factor projections, top-weight plots, heatmaps, and pathway enrichment together.

Re-rendering

Render the report from the repository root using the existing result tree:

Rscript MOFA-report-runner.R

Render the report and execute the MOFA-CORE.R function chain, skipping output blocks that already have mapped outputs:

Rscript MOFA-report-runner.R --run-analysis

Force a full rerun even when checkpoint outputs exist:

Rscript MOFA-report-runner.R --run-analysis --force

Use the updated normalisation branch rather than the legacy publication branch:

Rscript MOFA-report-runner.R --run-analysis --new-normalisation

The default report render reads the existing Results/ tree and creates directory-matched tabs. Analysis execution only occurs when --run-analysis is supplied.
